===== M0008 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.XYPlot.getDatasetsMappedToRangeAxis(Integer)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/XYPlot.java
Original range: 4352-4372
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method that returns a list of datasets that are mapped to a
     * particular axis.
     *
     * @param axisIndex  the axis index (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return A list of datasets.
     */
Method code:
4352 |     private List getDatasetsMappedToRangeAxis(Integer axisIndex) {
4353 |         if (axisIndex == null) {
4354 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'axisIndex' argument.");
4355 |         }
4356 |         List result = new ArrayList();
4357 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.datasets.size(); i++) {
4358 |             List mappedAxes = (List) this.datasetToRangeAxesMap.get(
4359 |                     new Integer(i));
4360 |             if (mappedAxes == null) {
4361 |                 if (axisIndex.equals(ZERO)) {
4362 |                     result.add(this.datasets.get(i));
4363 |                 }
4364 |             }
4365 |             else {
4366 |                 if (mappedAxes.contains(axisIndex)) {
4367 |                     result.add(this.datasets.get(i));
4368 |                 }
4369 |             }
4370 |         }
4371 |         return result;
4372 |     }

     * Notifies all registered listeners that the renderer has been modified.
     *

     */
Method code:
2903 |     public void notifyListeners(RendererChangeEvent event) {
2904 |         Object[] ls = this.listenerList.getListenerList();
2905 |         for (int i = ls.length - 2; i >= 0; i -= 2) {
2906 |             if (ls[i] == RendererChangeListener.class) {

Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeries.updateBoundsForAddedItem(XYDataItem)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/XYSeries.java

Documentation:
/**
     * Updates the cached values for the minimum and maximum data values.
     *
     * @param item  the item added (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @since 1.0.13
     */
Method code:
247 |     private void updateBoundsForAddedItem(XYDataItem item) {
248 |         double x = item.getXValue();
249 |         this.minX = minIgnoreNaN(this.minX, x);
250 |         this.maxX = maxIgnoreNaN(this.maxX, x);
251 |         if (item.getY() != null) {
252 |             double y = item.getYValue();
253 |             this.minY = minIgnoreNaN(this.minY, y);
254 |             this.maxY = maxIgnoreNaN(this.maxY, y);
255 |         }
256 |     }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.DateAxis.autoAdjustRange()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/DateAxis.java
Original range: 1275-1326
Documentation:
/**
     * Rescales the axis to ensure that all data is visible.
     */
Method code:
1275 |     protected void autoAdjustRange() {
1276 | 
1277 |         Plot plot = getPlot();
1278 | 
1279 |         if (plot == null) {
1280 |             return;  // no plot, no data
1281 |         }
1282 | 
1283 |         if (plot instanceof ValueAxisPlot) {
1284 |             ValueAxisPlot vap = (ValueAxisPlot) plot;
1285 | 
1286 |             Range r = vap.getDataRange(this);
1287 |             if (r == null) {
1288 |                 if (this.timeline instanceof SegmentedTimeline) {
1289 |                     //Timeline hasn't method getStartTime()
1290 |                     r = new DateRange((
1291 |                             (SegmentedTimeline) this.timeline).getStartTime(),
1292 |                             ((SegmentedTimeline) this.timeline).getStartTime()
1293 |                             + 1);
1294 |                 }
1295 |                 else {
1296 |                     r = new DateRange();
1297 |                 }
1298 |             }
1299 | 
1300 |             long upper = this.timeline.toTimelineValue(
1301 |                     (long) r.getUpperBound());
1302 |             long lower;
1303 |             long fixedAutoRange = (long) getFixedAutoRange();
1304 |             if (fixedAutoRange > 0.0) {
1305 |                 lower = upper - fixedAutoRange;
1306 |             }
1307 |             else {
1308 |                 lower = this.timeline.toTimelineValue((long) r.getLowerBound());
1309 |                 double range = upper - lower;
1310 |                 long minRange = (long) getAutoRangeMinimumSize();
1311 |                 if (range < minRange) {
1312 |                     long expand = (long) (minRange - range) / 2;
1313 |                     upper = upper + expand;
1314 |                     lower = lower - expand;
1315 |                 }
1316 |                 upper = upper + (long) (range * getUpperMargin());
1317 |                 lower = lower - (long) (range * getLowerMargin());
1318 |             }
1319 | 
1320 |             upper = this.timeline.toMillisecond(upper);
1321 |             lower = this.timeline.toMillisecond(lower);
1322 |             DateRange dr = new DateRange(new Date(lower), new Date(upper));
1323 |             setRange(dr, false, false);
1324 |         }
1325 | 
1326 |     }

===== M0060 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.Range.combine(Range,Range)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/Range.java
Original range: 213-229
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new range by combining two existing ranges.
     * <P>
     * Note that:
     * <ul>
     *   <li>either range can be <code>null</code>, in which case the other
     *       range is returned;</li>
     *   <li>if both ranges are <code>null</code> the return value is
     *       <code>null</code>.</li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * @param range1  the first range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param range2  the second range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     *
     * @return A new range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
213 |     public static Range combine(Range range1, Range range2) {
214 |         if (range1 == null) {
215 |             return range2;
216 |         }
217 |         else {
218 |             if (range2 == null) {
219 |                 return range1;
220 |             }
221 |             else {
222 |                 double l = Math.min(range1.getLowerBound(),
223 |                         range2.getLowerBound());
224 |                 double u = Math.max(range1.getUpperBound(),
225 |                         range2.getUpperBound());
226 |                 return new Range(l, u);
227 |             }
228 |         }
229 |     }

/**
     * Creates a new date instance.
     *
     * @param day  the day (in the range 1 to 28/29/30/31).
     * @param month  the month (in the range 1 to 12).
     * @param year  the year (in the range 1900 to 9999).
     */
Method code:
107 |     public SpreadsheetDate(final int day, final int month, final int year) {
108 | 
109 |         if ((year >= 1900) && (year <= 9999)) {
110 |             this.year = year;
111 |         }

113 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException(

125 |             );

===== M0066 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.TimeSeries.removeAgedItems(boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/TimeSeries.java
Original range: 873-891
Documentation:
/**
     * Age items in the series.  Ensure that the timespan from the youngest to
     * the oldest record in the series does not exceed maximumItemAge time
     * periods.  Oldest items will be removed if required.
     *
     * @param notify  controls whether or not a {@link SeriesChangeEvent} is
     *                sent to registered listeners IF any items are removed.
     */
Method code:
873 |     public void removeAgedItems(boolean notify) {
874 |         // check if there are any values earlier than specified by the history
875 |         // count...
876 |         if (getItemCount() > 1) {
877 |             long latest = getTimePeriod(getItemCount() - 1).getSerialIndex();
878 |             boolean removed = false;
879 |             while ((latest - getTimePeriod(0).getSerialIndex())
880 |                     > this.maximumItemAge) {
881 |                 this.data.remove(0);
882 |                 removed = true;
883 |             }
884 |             if (removed) {
885 |                 findBoundsByIteration();
886 |                 if (notify) {
887 |                     fireSeriesChanged();
888 |                 }
889 |             }
890 |         }
891 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.ChartFactory.createTimeSeriesChart(String,String,String,XYDataset,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/ChartFactory.java
Original range: 1880-1917
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates and returns a time series chart.  A time series chart is an
     * {@link XYPlot} with a {@link DateAxis} for the x-axis and a
     * {@link NumberAxis} for the y-axis.  The default renderer is an
     * {@link XYLineAndShapeRenderer}.
     * <P>
     * A convenient dataset to use with this chart is a
     * {@link org.jfree.data.time.TimeSeriesCollection}.
     *
     * @param title  the chart title (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param timeAxisLabel  a label for the time axis (<code>null</code>
     *                       permitted).
     * @param valueAxisLabel  a label for the value axis (<code>null</code>
     *                        permitted).
     * @param dataset  the dataset for the chart (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param legend  a flag specifying whether or not a legend is required.
     * @param tooltips  configure chart to generate tool tips?
     * @param urls  configure chart to generate URLs?
     *
     * @return A time series chart.
     */
Method code:
1880 |     public static JFreeChart createTimeSeriesChart(String title,
1881 |                                                    String timeAxisLabel,
1882 |                                                    String valueAxisLabel,
1883 |                                                    XYDataset dataset,
1884 |                                                    boolean legend,
1885 |                                                    boolean tooltips,
1886 |                                                    boolean urls) {
1887 | 
1888 |         ValueAxis timeAxis = new DateAxis(timeAxisLabel);
1889 |         timeAxis.setLowerMargin(0.02);  // reduce the default margins
1890 |         timeAxis.setUpperMargin(0.02);
1891 |         NumberAxis valueAxis = new NumberAxis(valueAxisLabel);
1892 |         valueAxis.setAutoRangeIncludesZero(false);  // override default
1893 |         XYPlot plot = new XYPlot(dataset, timeAxis, valueAxis, null);
1894 | 
1895 |         XYToolTipGenerator toolTipGenerator = null;
1896 |         if (tooltips) {
1897 |             toolTipGenerator
1898 |                 = StandardXYToolTipGenerator.getTimeSeriesInstance();
1899 |         }
1900 | 
1901 |         XYURLGenerator urlGenerator = null;
1902 |         if (urls) {
1903 |             urlGenerator = new StandardXYURLGenerator();
1904 |         }
1905 | 
1906 |         XYLineAndShapeRenderer renderer = new XYLineAndShapeRenderer(true,
1907 |                 false);
1908 |         renderer.setBaseToolTipGenerator(toolTipGenerator);
1909 |         renderer.setBaseURLGenerator(urlGenerator);
1910 |         plot.setRenderer(renderer);
1911 | 
1912 |         JFreeChart chart = new JFreeChart(title, JFreeChart.DEFAULT_TITLE_FONT,
1913 |                 plot, legend);
1914 |         currentTheme.apply(chart);
1915 |         return chart;
1916 | 
1917 |     }

===== M0072 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.TimeSeries.minIgnoreNaN(double,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/TimeSeries.java
Original range: 1265-1277
Documentation:
/**
     * A function to find the minimum of two values, but ignoring any
     * Double.NaN values.
     *
     * @param a  the first value.
     * @param b  the second value.
     *
     * @return The minimum of the two values.
     */
Method code:
1265 |     private double minIgnoreNaN(double a, double b) {
1266 |         if (Double.isNaN(a)) {
1267 |             return b;
1268 |         }
1269 |         else {
1270 |             if (Double.isNaN(b)) {
1271 |                 return a;
1272 |             }
1273 |             else {
1274 |                 return Math.min(a, b);
1275 |             }
1276 |         }
1277 |     }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.SpreadsheetDate.SpreadsheetDate(int)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/SpreadsheetDate.java
Original range: 146-201
Documentation:
/**
     * Standard constructor - creates a new date object representing the
     * specified day number (which should be in the range 2 to 2958465.
     *
     * @param serial  the serial number for the day (range: 2 to 2958465).
     */
Method code:
146 |     public SpreadsheetDate(final int serial) {
147 | 
148 |         if ((serial >= SERIAL_LOWER_BOUND) && (serial <= SERIAL_UPPER_BOUND)) {
149 |             this.serial = serial;
150 |         }
151 |         else {
152 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
153 |                 "SpreadsheetDate: Serial must be in range 2 to 2958465.");
154 |         }
155 | 
156 |         // the day-month-year needs to be synchronised with the serial number...
157 |       // get the year from the serial date
158 |       final int days = this.serial - SERIAL_LOWER_BOUND;
159 |       // overestimated because we ignored leap days
160 |       final int overestimatedYYYY = 1900 + (days / 365);
161 |       final int leaps = SerialDate.leapYearCount(overestimatedYYYY);
162 |       final int nonleapdays = days - leaps;
163 |       // underestimated because we overestimated years
164 |       int underestimatedYYYY = 1900 + (nonleapdays / 365);
165 | 
166 |       if (underestimatedYYYY == overestimatedYYYY) {
167 |           this.year = underestimatedYYYY;
168 |       }
169 |       else {
170 |           int ss1 = calcSerial(1, 1, underestimatedYYYY);
171 |           while (ss1 <= this.serial) {
172 |               underestimatedYYYY = underestimatedYYYY + 1;
173 |               ss1 = calcSerial(1, 1, underestimatedYYYY);
174 |           }
175 |           this.year = underestimatedYYYY - 1;
176 |       }
177 | 
178 |       final int ss2 = calcSerial(1, 1, this.year);
179 | 
180 |       int[] daysToEndOfPrecedingMonth
181 |           = AGGREGATE_DAYS_TO_END_OF_PRECEDING_MONTH;
182 | 
183 |       if (isLeapYear(this.year)) {
184 |           daysToEndOfPrecedingMonth
185 |               = LEAP_YEAR_AGGREGATE_DAYS_TO_END_OF_PRECEDING_MONTH;
186 |       }
187 | 
188 |       // get the month from the serial date
189 |       int mm = 1;
190 |       int sss = ss2 + daysToEndOfPrecedingMonth[mm] - 1;
191 |       while (sss < this.serial) {
192 |           mm = mm + 1;
193 |           sss = ss2 + daysToEndOfPrecedingMonth[mm] - 1;
194 |       }
195 |       this.month = mm - 1;
196 | 
197 |       // what's left is d(+1);
198 |       this.day = this.serial - ss2
199 |                  - daysToEndOfPrecedingMonth[this.month] + 1;
200 | 
201 |     }

===== M0102 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.SerialDate.leapYearCount(int)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/SerialDate.java
Original range: 524-531
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the number of leap years from 1900 to the specified year
     * INCLUSIVE.
     * <P>
     * Note that 1900 is not a leap year.
     *
     * @param yyyy  the year (in the range 1900 to 9999).
     *
     * @return the number of leap years from 1900 to the specified year.
     */
Method code:
524 |     public static int leapYearCount(final int yyyy) {
525 | 
526 |         final int leap4 = (yyyy - 1896) / 4;
527 |         final int leap100 = (yyyy - 1800) / 100;
528 |         final int leap400 = (yyyy - 1600) / 400;
529 |         return leap4 - leap100 + leap400;
530 | 
531 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.iterateToFindRangeBounds(XYDataset,List,Range,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 1368-1480
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of y-values in the specified dataset for the
     * data items belonging to the visible series and with x-values in the
     * given range.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param visibleSeriesKeys  the visible series keys (<code>null</code> not
     *     permitted).
     * @param xRange  the x-range (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines whether or not the
     *     y-interval for the dataset is included (this only applies if the
     *     dataset is an instance of IntervalXYDataset).
     *
     * @return The y-range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     *
     * @since 1.0.13
     */
Method code:
1368 |     public static Range iterateToFindRangeBounds(XYDataset dataset,
1369 |             List visibleSeriesKeys, Range xRange, boolean includeInterval) {
1370 | 
1371 |         if (dataset == null) {
1372 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'dataset' argument.");
1373 |         }
1374 |         if (visibleSeriesKeys == null) {
1375 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1376 |                     "Null 'visibleSeriesKeys' argument.");
1377 |         }
1378 |         if (xRange == null) {
1379 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'xRange' argument");
1380 |         }
1381 | 
1382 |         double minimum = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
1383 |         double maximum = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
1384 | 
1385 |         // handle three cases by dataset type
1386 |         if (includeInterval && dataset instanceof OHLCDataset) {
1387 |             // handle special case of OHLCDataset
1388 |             OHLCDataset ohlc = (OHLCDataset) dataset;
1389 |             Iterator iterator = visibleSeriesKeys.iterator();
1390 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1391 |                 Comparable seriesKey = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1392 |                 int series = dataset.indexOf(seriesKey);
1393 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
1394 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
1395 |                     double x = ohlc.getXValue(series, item);
1396 |                     if (xRange.contains(x)) {
1397 |                         double lvalue = ohlc.getLowValue(series, item);
1398 |                         double uvalue = ohlc.getHighValue(series, item);
1399 |                         if (!Double.isNaN(lvalue)) {
1400 |                             minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue);
1401 |                         }
1402 |                         if (!Double.isNaN(uvalue)) {
1403 |                             maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue);
1404 |                         }
1405 |                     }
1406 |                 }
1407 |             }
1408 |         }
1409 |         else if (includeInterval && dataset instanceof BoxAndWhiskerXYDataset) {
1410 |             // handle special case of BoxAndWhiskerXYDataset
1411 |             BoxAndWhiskerXYDataset bx = (BoxAndWhiskerXYDataset) dataset;
1412 |             Iterator iterator = visibleSeriesKeys.iterator();
1413 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1414 |                 Comparable seriesKey = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1415 |                 int series = dataset.indexOf(seriesKey);
1416 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
1417 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
1418 |                     double x = bx.getXValue(series, item);
1419 |                     if (xRange.contains(x)) {
1420 |                         Number lvalue = bx.getMinRegularValue(series, item);
1421 |                         Number uvalue = bx.getMaxRegularValue(series, item);
1422 |                         if (lvalue != null) {
1423 |                             minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue.doubleValue());
1424 |                         }
1425 |                         if (uvalue != null) {
1426 |                             maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue.doubleValue());
1427 |                         }
1428 |                     }
1429 |                 }
1430 |             }
1431 |         }
1432 |         else if (includeInterval && dataset instanceof IntervalXYDataset) {
1433 |             // handle special case of IntervalXYDataset
1434 |             IntervalXYDataset ixyd = (IntervalXYDataset) dataset;
1435 |             Iterator iterator = visibleSeriesKeys.iterator();
1436 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1437 |                 Comparable seriesKey = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1438 |                 int series = dataset.indexOf(seriesKey);
1439 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
1440 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
1441 |                     double x = ixyd.getXValue(series, item);
1442 |                     if (xRange.contains(x)) {
1443 |                         double lvalue = ixyd.getStartYValue(series, item);
1444 |                         double uvalue = ixyd.getEndYValue(series, item);
1445 |                         if (!Double.isNaN(lvalue)) {
1446 |                             minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue);
1447 |                         }
1448 |                         if (!Double.isNaN(uvalue)) {
1449 |                             maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue);
1450 |                         }
1451 |                     }
1452 |                 }
1453 |             }
1454 |         }
1455 |         else {
1456 |             // standard case - plain XYDataset
1457 |             Iterator iterator = visibleSeriesKeys.iterator();
1458 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1459 |                 Comparable seriesKey = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1460 |                 int series = dataset.indexOf(seriesKey);
1461 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
1462 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
1463 |                     double x = dataset.getXValue(series, item);
1464 |                     double y = dataset.getYValue(series, item);
1465 |                     if (xRange.contains(x)) {
1466 |                         if (!Double.isNaN(y)) {
1467 |                             minimum = Math.min(minimum, y);
1468 |                             maximum = Math.max(maximum, y);
1469 |                         }
1470 |                     }
1471 |                 }
1472 |             }
1473 |         }
1474 |         if (minimum == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
1475 |             return null;
1476 |         }
1477 |         else {
1478 |             return new Range(minimum, maximum);
1479 |         }
1480 |     }

===== M0129 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.TimeSeriesCollection.getDomainBounds(boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/TimeSeriesCollection.java
Original range: 635-657
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of the values in this dataset's domain.
     *
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines whether or not the
     *                         x-interval is taken into account.
     *
     * @return The range.
     */
Method code:
635 |     public Range getDomainBounds(boolean includeInterval) {
636 |         Range result = null;
637 |         Iterator iterator = this.data.iterator();
638 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
639 |             TimeSeries series = (TimeSeries) iterator.next();
640 |             int count = series.getItemCount();
641 |             if (count > 0) {
642 |                 RegularTimePeriod start = series.getTimePeriod(0);
643 |                 RegularTimePeriod end = series.getTimePeriod(count - 1);
644 |                 Range temp;
645 |                 if (!includeInterval) {
646 |                     temp = new Range(getX(start), getX(end));
647 |                 }
648 |                 else {
649 |                     temp = new Range(
650 |                             start.getFirstMillisecond(this.workingCalendar),
651 |                             end.getLastMillisecond(this.workingCalendar));
652 |                 }
653 |                 result = Range.combine(result, temp);
654 |             }
655 |         }
656 |         return result;
657 |     }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.iterateToFindDomainBounds(XYDataset,List,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 1292-1349
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of x-values in the specified dataset for the
     * data items belonging to the visible series.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param visibleSeriesKeys  the visible series keys (<code>null</code> not
     *     permitted).
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines whether or not the
     *     y-interval for the dataset is included (this only applies if the
     *     dataset is an instance of IntervalXYDataset).
     *
     * @return The x-range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     *
     * @since 1.0.13
     */
Method code:
1292 |     public static Range iterateToFindDomainBounds(XYDataset dataset,
1293 |             List visibleSeriesKeys, boolean includeInterval) {
1294 | 
1295 |         if (dataset == null) {
1296 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'dataset' argument.");
1297 |         }
1298 |         if (visibleSeriesKeys == null) {
1299 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1300 |                     "Null 'visibleSeriesKeys' argument.");
1301 |         }
1302 | 
1303 |         double minimum = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
1304 |         double maximum = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
1305 | 
1306 |         if (includeInterval && dataset instanceof IntervalXYDataset) {
1307 |             // handle special case of IntervalXYDataset
1308 |             IntervalXYDataset ixyd = (IntervalXYDataset) dataset;
1309 |             Iterator iterator = visibleSeriesKeys.iterator();
1310 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1311 |                 Comparable seriesKey = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1312 |                 int series = dataset.indexOf(seriesKey);
1313 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
1314 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
1315 |                     double lvalue = ixyd.getStartXValue(series, item);
1316 |                     double uvalue = ixyd.getEndXValue(series, item);
1317 |                     if (!Double.isNaN(lvalue)) {
1318 |                         minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue);
1319 |                     }
1320 |                     if (!Double.isNaN(uvalue)) {
1321 |                         maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue);
1322 |                     }
1323 |                 }
1324 |             }
1325 |         }
1326 |         else {
1327 |             // standard case - plain XYDataset
1328 |             Iterator iterator = visibleSeriesKeys.iterator();
1329 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1330 |                 Comparable seriesKey = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1331 |                 int series = dataset.indexOf(seriesKey);
1332 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
1333 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
1334 |                     double x = dataset.getXValue(series, item);
1335 |                     if (!Double.isNaN(x)) {
1336 |                         minimum = Math.min(minimum, x);
1337 |                         maximum = Math.max(maximum, x);
1338 |                     }
1339 |                 }
1340 |             }
1341 |         }
1342 | 
1343 |         if (minimum == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
1344 |             return null;
1345 |         }
1346 |         else {
1347 |             return new Range(minimum, maximum);
1348 |         }
1349 |     }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.IntervalXYDelegate.getIntervalWidth()
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/IntervalXYDelegate.java
Original range: 239-249
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the interval width.  This method will return either the
     * auto calculated interval width or the manually specified interval
     * width, depending on the {@link #isAutoWidth()} result.
     *
     * @return The interval width to use.
     */
Method code:
239 |     public double getIntervalWidth() {
240 |         if (isAutoWidth() && !Double.isInfinite(this.autoIntervalWidth)) {
241 |             // everything is fine: autoWidth is on, and an autoIntervalWidth
242 |             // was set.
243 |             return this.autoIntervalWidth;

===== M0151 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.SpreadsheetDate.calcSerial(int,int,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/SpreadsheetDate.java
Original range: 448-458
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculate the serial number from the day, month and year.
     * <P>
     * 1-Jan-1900 = 2.
     *
     * @param d  the day.
     * @param m  the month.
     * @param y  the year.
     *
     * @return the serial number from the day, month and year.
     */
Method code:
448 |     private int calcSerial(final int d, final int m, final int y) {
449 |         final int yy = ((y - 1900) * 365) + SerialDate.leapYearCount(y - 1);
450 |         int mm = SerialDate.AGGREGATE_DAYS_TO_END_OF_PRECEDING_MONTH[m];
451 |         if (m > MonthConstants.FEBRUARY) {
452 |             if (SerialDate.isLeapYear(y)) {
453 |                 mm = mm + 1;
454 |             }
455 |         }
456 |         final int dd = d;
457 |         return yy + mm + dd + 1;
458 |     }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.TimeSeries.add(TimeSeriesDataItem,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/TimeSeries.java
Original range: 574-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Adds a data item to the series and sends a {@link SeriesChangeEvent} to
     * all registered listeners.
     *
     * @param item  the (timeperiod, value) pair (<code>null</code> not
     *              permitted).
     * @param notify  notify listeners?
     */
Method code:
574 |     public void add(TimeSeriesDataItem item, boolean notify) {
575 |         if (item == null) {
576 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'item' argument.");
577 |         }
578 |         item = (TimeSeriesDataItem) item.clone();
579 |         Class c = item.getPeriod().getClass();
580 |         if (this.timePeriodClass == null) {
581 |             this.timePeriodClass = c;
582 |         }
583 |         else if (!this.timePeriodClass.equals(c)) {
584 |             StringBuffer b = new StringBuffer();
585 |             b.append("You are trying to add data where the time period class ");
586 |             b.append("is ");
587 |             b.append(item.getPeriod().getClass().getName());
588 |             b.append(", but the TimeSeries is expecting an instance of ");
589 |             b.append(this.timePeriodClass.getName());
590 |             b.append(".");
591 |             throw new SeriesException(b.toString());
592 |         }
593 | 
594 |         // make the change (if it's not a duplicate time period)...
595 |         boolean added = false;
596 |         int count = getItemCount();
597 |         if (count == 0) {
598 |             this.data.add(item);
599 |             added = true;
600 |         }
601 |         else {
602 |             RegularTimePeriod last = getTimePeriod(getItemCount() - 1);
603 |             if (item.getPeriod().compareTo(last) > 0) {
604 |                 this.data.add(item);
605 |                 added = true;
606 |             }
607 |             else {
608 |                 int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.data, item);
609 |                 if (index < 0) {
610 |                     this.data.add(-index - 1, item);
611 |                     added = true;
612 |                 }
613 |                 else {
614 |                     StringBuffer b = new StringBuffer();
615 |                     b.append("You are attempting to add an observation for ");
616 |                     b.append("the time period ");
617 |                     b.append(item.getPeriod().toString());
618 |                     b.append(" but the series already contains an observation");
619 |                     b.append(" for that time period. Duplicates are not ");
620 |                     b.append("permitted.  Try using the addOrUpdate() method.");
621 |                     throw new SeriesException(b.toString());
622 |                 }
623 |             }
624 |         }
625 |         if (added) {
626 |             updateBoundsForAddedItem(item);
627 |             // check if this addition will exceed the maximum item count...
628 |             if (getItemCount() > this.maximumItemCount) {
629 |                 TimeSeriesDataItem d = (TimeSeriesDataItem) this.data.remove(0);
630 |                 updateBoundsForRemovedItem(d);
631 |             }
632 | 
633 |             removeAgedItems(false);  // remove old items if necessary, but
634 |                                      // don't notify anyone, because that
635 |                                      // happens next anyway...
636 |             if (notify) {
637 |                 fireSeriesChanged();
638 |             }
639 |         }
640 | 
641 |     }

     * calendar (which determines the time zone).

===== M0190 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.autoAdjustRange()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 424-510
Documentation:
/**
     * Rescales the axis to ensure that all data is visible.
     */
Method code:
424 |     protected void autoAdjustRange() {
425 | 
426 |         Plot plot = getPlot();
427 |         if (plot == null) {
428 |             return;  // no plot, no data
429 |         }
430 | 
431 |         if (plot instanceof ValueAxisPlot) {
432 |             ValueAxisPlot vap = (ValueAxisPlot) plot;
433 | 
434 |             Range r = vap.getDataRange(this);
435 |             if (r == null) {
436 |                 r = getDefaultAutoRange();
437 |             }
438 | 
439 |             double upper = r.getUpperBound();
440 |             double lower = r.getLowerBound();
441 |             if (this.rangeType == RangeType.POSITIVE) {
442 |                 lower = Math.max(0.0, lower);
443 |                 upper = Math.max(0.0, upper);
444 |             }
445 |             else if (this.rangeType == RangeType.NEGATIVE) {
446 |                 lower = Math.min(0.0, lower);
447 |                 upper = Math.min(0.0, upper);
448 |             }
449 | 
450 |             if (getAutoRangeIncludesZero()) {
451 |                 lower = Math.min(lower, 0.0);
452 |                 upper = Math.max(upper, 0.0);
453 |             }
454 |             double range = upper - lower;
455 | 
456 |             // if fixed auto range, then derive lower bound...
457 |             double fixedAutoRange = getFixedAutoRange();
458 |             if (fixedAutoRange > 0.0) {
459 |                 lower = upper - fixedAutoRange;
460 |             }
461 |             else {
462 |                 // ensure the autorange is at least <minRange> in size...
463 |                 double minRange = getAutoRangeMinimumSize();
464 |                 if (range < minRange) {
465 |                     double expand = (minRange - range) / 2;
466 |                     upper = upper + expand;
467 |                     lower = lower - expand;
468 |                     if (lower == upper) { // see bug report 1549218
469 |                         double adjust = Math.abs(lower) / 10.0;
470 |                         lower = lower - adjust;
471 |                         upper = upper + adjust;
472 |                     }
473 |                     if (this.rangeType == RangeType.POSITIVE) {
474 |                         if (lower < 0.0) {
475 |                             upper = upper - lower;
476 |                             lower = 0.0;
477 |                         }
478 |                     }
479 |                     else if (this.rangeType == RangeType.NEGATIVE) {
480 |                         if (upper > 0.0) {
481 |                             lower = lower - upper;
482 |                             upper = 0.0;
483 |                         }
484 |                     }
485 |                 }
486 | 
487 |                 if (getAutoRangeStickyZero()) {
488 |                     if (upper <= 0.0) {
489 |                         upper = Math.min(0.0, upper + getUpperMargin() * range);
490 |                     }
491 |                     else {
492 |                         upper = upper + getUpperMargin() * range;
493 |                     }
494 |                     if (lower >= 0.0) {
495 |                         lower = Math.max(0.0, lower - getLowerMargin() * range);
496 |                     }
497 |                     else {
498 |                         lower = lower - getLowerMargin() * range;
499 |                     }
500 |                 }
501 |                 else {
502 |                     upper = upper + getUpperMargin() * range;
503 |                     lower = lower - getLowerMargin() * range;
504 |                 }
505 |             }
506 | 
507 |             setRange(new Range(lower, upper), false, false);
508 |         }
509 | 
510 |     }

===== M0192 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeriesCollection.getDomainBounds(boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/XYSeriesCollection.java
Original range: 576-602
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of the values in this dataset's domain.
     *
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines whether or not the
     *                         x-interval is taken into account.
     *
     * @return The range (or <code>null</code> if the dataset contains no
     *     values).
     */
Method code:
576 |     public Range getDomainBounds(boolean includeInterval) {
577 |         if (includeInterval) {
578 |             return this.intervalDelegate.getDomainBounds(includeInterval);
579 |         }
580 |         else {
581 |             double lower = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
582 |             double upper = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
583 |             int seriesCount = getSeriesCount();
584 |             for (int s = 0; s < seriesCount; s++) {
585 |                 XYSeries series = getSeries(s);
586 |                 double minX = series.getMinX();
587 |                 if (!Double.isNaN(minX)) {
588 |                     lower = Math.min(lower, minX);
589 |                 }
590 |                 double maxX = series.getMaxX();
591 |                 if (!Double.isNaN(maxX)) {
592 |                     upper = Math.max(upper, maxX);
593 |                 }
594 |             }
595 |             if (lower > upper) {
596 |                 return null;
597 |             }
598 |             else {
599 |                 return new Range(lower, upper);
600 |             }
601 |         }
602 |     }

     * @param xAxisLabel  a label for the X-axis (<code>null</code> permitted).

     * @param dataset  the dataset for the chart (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param orientation  the plot orientation (horizontal or vertical)
     *                     (<code>null</code> NOT permitted).
     * @param legend  a flag specifying whether or not a legend is required.
     * @param tooltips  configure chart to generate tool tips?
     * @param urls  configure chart to generate URLs?
     *
     * @return The chart.
     */
Method code:
1717 |     public static JFreeChart createXYLineChart(String title,
1718 |                                                String xAxisLabel,
1719 |                                                String yAxisLabel,
1720 |                                                XYDataset dataset,
1721 |                                                PlotOrientation orientation,
1722 |                                                boolean legend,
1723 |                                                boolean tooltips,
1724 |                                                boolean urls) {
1725 | 
1726 |         if (orientation == null) {
1727 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
1728 |         }
1729 |         NumberAxis xAxis = new NumberAxis(xAxisLabel);
1730 |         xAxis.setAutoRangeIncludesZero(false);
1731 |         NumberAxis yAxis = new NumberAxis(yAxisLabel);
1732 |         XYItemRenderer renderer = new XYLineAndShapeRenderer(true, false);
1733 |         XYPlot plot = new XYPlot(dataset, xAxis, yAxis, renderer);
1734 |         plot.setOrientation(orientation);
1735 |         if (tooltips) {
1736 |             renderer.setBaseToolTipGenerator(new StandardXYToolTipGenerator());
1737 |         }
1738 |         if (urls) {
1739 |             renderer.setBaseURLGenerator(new StandardXYURLGenerator());
1740 |         }
1741 | 
1742 |         JFreeChart chart = new JFreeChart(title, JFreeChart.DEFAULT_TITLE_FONT,
1743 |                 plot, legend);
1744 |         currentTheme.apply(chart);
1745 |         return chart;
1746 | 
1747 |     }

===== M0208 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.IntervalXYDelegate.getDomainBounds(boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/IntervalXYDelegate.java
Original range: 364-375
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of the values in the dataset's domain, including
     * or excluding the interval around each x-value as specified.
     *
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines whether or not the
     *                         x-interval should be taken into account.
     *
     * @return The range.
     */
Method code:
364 |     public Range getDomainBounds(boolean includeInterval) {
365 |         // first get the range without the interval, then expand it for the
366 |         // interval width
367 |         Range range = DatasetUtilities.findDomainBounds(this.dataset, false);
368 |         if (includeInterval && range != null) {
369 |             double lowerAdj = getIntervalWidth() * getIntervalPositionFactor();
370 |             double upperAdj = getIntervalWidth() - lowerAdj;
371 |             range = new Range(range.getLowerBound() - lowerAdj,
372 |                 range.getUpperBound() + upperAdj);
373 |         }
374 |         return range;
375 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeries.maxIgnoreNaN(double,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/XYSeries.java
Original range: 721-733
Documentation:
/**
     * A function to find the maximum of two values, but ignoring any
     * Double.NaN values.
     *
     * @param a  the first value.
     * @param b  the second value.
     *
     * @return The maximum of the two values.
     */
Method code:
721 |     private double maxIgnoreNaN(double a, double b) {
722 |         if (Double.isNaN(a)) {
723 |             return b;
724 |         }
725 |         else {
726 |             if (Double.isNaN(b)) {
727 |                 return a;
728 |             }
729 |             else {
730 |                 return Math.max(a, b);
731 |             }
732 |         }
733 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the lower and upper bounds (range) of the x-values in the
     * specified dataset.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param includeInterval  include the interval (if any) for the dataset?
     *
     * @return The range (<code>null</code> if the dataset is <code>null</code>
     *         or empty).
     *
     * @since 1.0.13
     */
Method code:
824 |     protected Range findDomainBounds(XYDataset dataset,
825 |             boolean includeInterval) {
826 |         if (dataset == null) {
827 |             return null;
828 |         }
829 |         if (getDataBoundsIncludesVisibleSeriesOnly()) {
830 |             List visibleSeriesKeys = new ArrayList();
831 |             int seriesCount = dataset.getSeriesCount();
832 |             for (int s = 0; s < seriesCount; s++) {
833 |                 if (isSeriesVisible(s)) {
834 |                     visibleSeriesKeys.add(dataset.getSeriesKey(s));
835 |                 }
836 |             }
837 |             return DatasetUtilities.findDomainBounds(dataset,
838 |                     visibleSeriesKeys, includeInterval);
839 |         }
840 |         else {
841 |             return DatasetUtilities.findDomainBounds(dataset, includeInterval);
842 |         }
843 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * Sets the upper margin for the axis (as a percentage of the axis range)
     * and sends an {@link AxisChangeEvent} to all registered listeners.  This
     * margin is added only when the axis range is auto-calculated - if you set
     * the axis range manually, the margin is ignored.
     *
     * @param margin  the margin percentage (for example, 0.05 is five percent).
     *
     * @see #getLowerMargin()
     * @see #setLowerMargin(double)
     */
Method code:
1128 |     public void setUpperMargin(double margin) {
1129 |         this.upperMargin = margin;
1130 |         if (isAutoRange()) {
1131 |             autoAdjustRange();
1132 |         }
1133 |         notifyListeners(new AxisChangeEvent(this));
1134 |     }

===== M0285 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.Day.compareTo(Object)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/Day.java
Original range: 402-429
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns an integer indicating the order of this Day object relative to
     * the specified object:
     *
     * negative == before, zero == same, positive == after.
     *
     * @param o1  the object to compare.
     *
     * @return negative == before, zero == same, positive == after.
     */
Method code:
402 |     public int compareTo(Object o1) {
403 | 
404 |         int result;
405 | 
406 |         // CASE 1 : Comparing to another Day object
407 |         // ----------------------------------------
408 |         if (o1 instanceof Day) {
409 |             Day d = (Day) o1;
410 |             result = -d.getSerialDate().compare(this.serialDate);
411 |         }
412 | 
413 |         // CASE 2 : Comparing to another TimePeriod object
414 |         // -----------------------------------------------
415 |         else if (o1 instanceof RegularTimePeriod) {
416 |             // more difficult case - evaluate later...
417 |             result = 0;
418 |         }
419 | 
420 |         // CASE 3 : Comparing to a non-TimePeriod object
421 |         // ---------------------------------------------
422 |         else {
423 |             // consider time periods to be ordered after general objects
424 |             result = 1;
425 |         }
426 | 
427 |         return result;
428 | 
429 |     }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeries.add(XYDataItem,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/XYSeries.java
Original range: 503-552
Documentation:
/**
     * Adds a data item to the series and, if requested, sends a
     * {@link SeriesChangeEvent} to all registered listeners.
     *
     * @param item  the (x, y) item (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param notify  a flag that controls whether or not a
     *                {@link SeriesChangeEvent} is sent to all registered
     *                listeners.
     */
Method code:
503 |     public void add(XYDataItem item, boolean notify) {
504 |         if (item == null) {
505 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'item' argument.");
506 |         }
507 |         item = (XYDataItem) item.clone();
508 |         if (this.autoSort) {
509 |             int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.data, item);
510 |             if (index < 0) {
511 |                 this.data.add(-index - 1, item);
512 |             }
513 |             else {
514 |                 if (this.allowDuplicateXValues) {
515 |                     // need to make sure we are adding *after* any duplicates
516 |                     int size = this.data.size();
517 |                     while (index < size && item.compareTo(
518 |                             this.data.get(index)) == 0) {
519 |                         index++;
520 |                     }
521 |                     if (index < this.data.size()) {
522 |                         this.data.add(index, item);
523 |                     }
524 |                     else {
525 |                         this.data.add(item);
526 |                     }
527 |                 }
528 |                 else {
529 |                     throw new SeriesException("X-value already exists.");
530 |                 }
531 |             }
532 |         }
533 |         else {
534 |             if (!this.allowDuplicateXValues) {
535 |                 // can't allow duplicate values, so we need to check whether
536 |                 // there is an item with the given x-value already
537 |                 int index = indexOf(item.getX());
538 |                 if (index >= 0) {
539 |                     throw new SeriesException("X-value already exists.");
540 |                 }
541 |             }
542 |             this.data.add(item);
543 |         }
544 |         updateBoundsForAddedItem(item);
545 |         if (getItemCount() > this.maximumItemCount) {
546 |             XYDataItem removed = (XYDataItem) this.data.remove(0);
547 |             updateBoundsForRemovedItem(removed);
548 |         }
549 |         if (notify) {
550 |             fireSeriesChanged();
551 |         }
552 |     }

/**
     * Compares this tick unit to an arbitrary object.
     *
     * @param object  the object to compare against.
     *
     * @return <code>1</code> if the size of the other object is less than this,
     *      <code>0</code> if both have the same size and <code>-1</code> this

     * @param urls  configure chart to generate URLs?
     *
     * @return A scatter plot.

1502 |         renderer.setBaseToolTipGenerator(toolTipGenerator);

1504 |         plot.setRenderer(renderer);

Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.XYSeries.minIgnoreNaN(double,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/XYSeries.java
Original range: 698-710
Documentation:
/**
     * A function to find the minimum of two values, but ignoring any
     * Double.NaN values.
     *
     * @param a  the first value.
     * @param b  the second value.
     *
     * @return The minimum of the two values.
     */
Method code:
698 |     private double minIgnoreNaN(double a, double b) {
699 |         if (Double.isNaN(a)) {
700 |             return b;
701 |         }
702 |         else {
703 |             if (Double.isNaN(b)) {
704 |                 return a;
705 |             }
706 |             else {
707 |                 return Math.min(a, b);
708 |             }
709 |         }
710 |     }

3817 |         if (axisIndices != null) {
3818 |             // the first axis in the list is used for data <--> Java2D
3819 |             Integer axisIndex = (Integer) axisIndices.get(0);
3820 |             valueAxis = getDomainAxis(axisIndex.intValue());

3822 |         else {

3824 |         }

/**
     * Creates an array of standard shapes to display for the items in series
     * on charts.

Method code:
287 |     public static Shape[] createStandardSeriesShapes() {
288 | 
289 |         Shape[] result = new Shape[10];
290 |

293 |         int[] xpoints = null;

309 |         result[3] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 4);
310 |

312 |         result[4] = new Rectangle2D.Double(-delta, -delta / 2, size, size / 2);
313 | 
314 |         // down-pointing triangle
315 |         xpoints = intArray(-delta, +delta, 0.0);
316 |         ypoints = intArray(-delta, -delta, delta);
317 |         result[5] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
318 | 
319 |         // horizontal ellipse
320 |         result[6] = new Ellipse2D.Double(-delta, -delta / 2, size, size / 2);
321 | 
322 |         // right-pointing triangle
323 |         xpoints = intArray(-delta, delta, -delta);
324 |         ypoints = intArray(-delta, 0.0, delta);
325 |         result[7] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
326 | 
327 |         // vertical rectangle
328 |         result[8] = new Rectangle2D.Double(-delta / 2, -delta, size / 2, size);
329 | 
330 |         // left-pointing triangle
331 |         xpoints = intArray(-delta, delta, delta);
332 |         ypoints = intArray(0.0, -delta, +delta);
333 |         result[9] = new Polygon(xpoints, ypoints, 3);
334 |

===== M0313 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.XYPlot.checkAxisIndices(List)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/XYPlot.java
Original range: 1532-1555
Documentation:
/**
     * This method is used to perform argument checking on the list of
     * axis indices passed to mapDatasetToDomainAxes() and
     * mapDatasetToRangeAxes().
     *
     * @param indices  the list of indices (<code>null</code> permitted).
     */
Method code:
1532 |     private void checkAxisIndices(List indices) {
1533 |         // axisIndices can be:
1534 |         // 1.  null;
1535 |         // 2.  non-empty, containing only Integer objects that are unique.
1536 |         if (indices == null) {
1537 |             return;  // OK
1538 |         }
1539 |         int count = indices.size();
1540 |         if (count == 0) {
1541 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Empty list not permitted.");
1542 |         }
1543 |         HashSet set = new HashSet();
1544 |         for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
1545 |             Object item = indices.get(i);
1546 |             if (!(item instanceof Integer)) {
1547 |                 throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1548 |                         "Indices must be Integer instances.");
1549 |             }
1550 |             if (set.contains(item)) {
1551 |                 throw new IllegalArgumentException("Indices must be unique.");
1552 |             }
1553 |             set.add(item);
1554 |         }
1555 |     }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.xy.XYDataItem.compareTo(Object)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/xy/XYDataItem.java
Original range: 212-244
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns an integer indicating the order of this object relative to
     * another object.
     * <P>
     * For the order we consider only the x-value:
     * negative == "less-than", zero == "equal", positive == "greater-than".
     *
     * @param o1  the object being compared to.
     *
     * @return An integer indicating the order of this data pair object
     *      relative to another object.
     */
Method code:
212 |     public int compareTo(Object o1) {
213 | 
214 |         int result;
215 | 
216 |         // CASE 1 : Comparing to another TimeSeriesDataPair object
217 |         // -------------------------------------------------------
218 |         if (o1 instanceof XYDataItem) {
219 |             XYDataItem dataItem = (XYDataItem) o1;
220 |             double compare = this.x.doubleValue()
221 |                              - dataItem.getX().doubleValue();
222 |             if (compare > 0.0) {
223 |                 result = 1;
224 |             }
225 |             else {
226 |                 if (compare < 0.0) {
227 |                     result = -1;
228 |                 }
229 |                 else {
230 |                     result = 0;
231 |                 }
232 |             }
233 |         }
234 | 
235 |         // CASE 2 : Comparing to a general object
236 |         // ---------------------------------------------
237 |         else {
238 |             // consider time periods to be ordered after general objects
239 |             result = 1;
240 |         }
241 | 
242 |         return result;
243 | 
244 |     }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.StandardChartTheme.applyToPlot(Plot)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/StandardChartTheme.java
Original range: 1235-1278
Documentation:
/**
     * Applies the attributes of this theme to a plot.
     *
     * @param plot  the plot (<code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
1235 |     protected void applyToPlot(Plot plot) {
1236 |         if (plot == null) {
1237 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'plot' argument.");
1238 |         }
1239 |         if (plot.getDrawingSupplier() != null) {
1240 |             plot.setDrawingSupplier(getDrawingSupplier());
1241 |         }
1242 |         if (plot.getBackgroundPaint() != null) {
1243 |             plot.setBackgroundPaint(this.plotBackgroundPaint);
1244 |         }
1245 |         plot.setOutlinePaint(this.plotOutlinePaint);
1246 | 
1247 |         // now handle specific plot types (and yes, I know this is some
1248 |         // really ugly code that has to be manually updated any time a new
1249 |         // plot type is added - I should have written something much cooler,
1250 |         // but I didn't and neither did anyone else).
1251 |         if (plot instanceof PiePlot) {
1252 |             applyToPiePlot((PiePlot) plot);
1253 |         }
1254 |         else if (plot instanceof MultiplePiePlot) {
1255 |             applyToMultiplePiePlot((MultiplePiePlot) plot);
1256 |         }
1257 |         else if (plot instanceof CategoryPlot) {
1258 |             applyToCategoryPlot((CategoryPlot) plot);
1259 |         }
1260 |         else if (plot instanceof XYPlot) {
1261 |             applyToXYPlot((XYPlot) plot);
1262 |         }
1263 |         else if (plot instanceof FastScatterPlot) {
1264 |             applyToFastScatterPlot((FastScatterPlot) plot);
1265 |         }
1266 |         else if (plot instanceof MeterPlot) {
1267 |             applyToMeterPlot((MeterPlot) plot);
1268 |         }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.DateTickUnit.getMillisecondCount(DateTickUnitType,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/DateTickUnit.java
Original range: 301-329
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the (approximate) number of milliseconds for the given unit and
     * unit count.
     * <P>
     * This value is an approximation some of the time (e.g. months are
     * assumed to have 31 days) but this shouldn't matter.
     *
     * @param unit  the unit.
     * @param count  the unit count.
     *
     * @return The number of milliseconds.
     *
     * @since 1.0.13
     */
Method code:
301 |     private static long getMillisecondCount(DateTickUnitType unit, int count) {
302 | 
303 |         if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.YEAR)) {
304 |             return (365L * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
305 |         }
306 |         else if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.MONTH)) {
307 |             return (31L * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
308 |         }
309 |         else if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.DAY)) {
310 |             return (24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
311 |         }
312 |         else if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.HOUR)) {
313 |             return (60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
314 |         }
315 |         else if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.MINUTE)) {
316 |             return (60L * 1000L) * count;
317 |         }
318 |         else if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.SECOND)) {
319 |             return 1000L * count;
320 |         }
321 |         else if (unit.equals(DateTickUnitType.MILLISECOND)) {
322 |             return count;
323 |         }
324 |         else {
325 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("The 'unit' argument has a " +
326 |                     "value that is not recognised.");
327 |         }
328 | 
329 |     }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.TimeSeries.maxIgnoreNaN(double,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/TimeSeries.java
Original range: 1288-1300
Documentation:
/**
     * A function to find the maximum of two values, but ignoring any
     * Double.NaN values.
     *
     * @param a  the first value.
     * @param b  the second value.
     *
     * @return The maximum of the two values.
     */
Method code:
1288 |     private double maxIgnoreNaN(double a, double b) {
1289 |         if (Double.isNaN(a)) {
1290 |             return b;
1291 |         }
1292 |         else {
1293 |             if (Double.isNaN(b)) {
1294 |                 return a;
1295 |             }
1296 |             else {
1297 |                 return Math.max(a, b);
1298 |             }
1299 |         }
1300 |     }

===== M0372 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.XYPlot.getDataRange(ValueAxis)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/XYPlot.java
Original range: 4425-4519
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range for the specified axis.
     *
     * @param axis  the axis.
     *
     * @return The range.
     */
Method code:
4425 |     public Range getDataRange(ValueAxis axis) {
4426 | 
4427 |         Range result = null;
4428 |         List mappedDatasets = new ArrayList();
4429 |         List includedAnnotations = new ArrayList();
4430 |         boolean isDomainAxis = true;
4431 | 
4432 |         // is it a domain axis?
4433 |         int domainIndex = getDomainAxisIndex(axis);
4434 |         if (domainIndex >= 0) {
4435 |             isDomainAxis = true;
4436 |             mappedDatasets.addAll(getDatasetsMappedToDomainAxis(
4437 |                     new Integer(domainIndex)));
4438 |             if (domainIndex == 0) {
4439 |                 // grab the plot's annotations
4440 |                 Iterator iterator = this.annotations.iterator();
4441 |                 while (iterator.hasNext()) {
4442 |                     XYAnnotation annotation = (XYAnnotation) iterator.next();
4443 |                     if (annotation instanceof XYAnnotationBoundsInfo) {
4444 |                         includedAnnotations.add(annotation);
4445 |                     }
4446 |                 }
4447 |             }
4448 |         }
4449 | 
4450 |         // or is it a range axis?
4451 |         int rangeIndex = getRangeAxisIndex(axis);
4452 |         if (rangeIndex >= 0) {
4453 |             isDomainAxis = false;
4454 |             mappedDatasets.addAll(getDatasetsMappedToRangeAxis(
4455 |                     new Integer(rangeIndex)));
4456 |             if (rangeIndex == 0) {
4457 |                 Iterator iterator = this.annotations.iterator();
4458 |                 while (iterator.hasNext()) {
4459 |                     XYAnnotation annotation = (XYAnnotation) iterator.next();
4460 |                     if (annotation instanceof XYAnnotationBoundsInfo) {
4461 |                         includedAnnotations.add(annotation);
4462 |                     }
4463 |                 }
4464 |             }
4465 |         }
4466 | 
4467 |         // iterate through the datasets that map to the axis and get the union
4468 |         // of the ranges.
4469 |         Iterator iterator = mappedDatasets.iterator();
4470 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
4471 |             XYDataset d = (XYDataset) iterator.next();
4472 |             if (d != null) {
4473 |                 XYItemRenderer r = getRendererForDataset(d);
4474 |                 if (isDomainAxis) {
4475 |                     if (r != null) {
4476 |                         result = Range.combine(result, r.findDomainBounds(d));
4477 |                     }
4478 |                     else {
4479 |                         result = Range.combine(result,
4480 |                                 DatasetUtilities.findDomainBounds(d));
4481 |                     }
4482 |                 }
4483 |                 else {
4484 |                     if (r != null) {
4485 |                         result = Range.combine(result, r.findRangeBounds(d));
4486 |                     }
4487 |                     else {
4488 |                         result = Range.combine(result,
4489 |                                 DatasetUtilities.findRangeBounds(d));
4490 |                     }
4491 |                 }
4492 |                 
4493 |                     Collection c = r.getAnnotations();
4494 |                     Iterator i = c.iterator();
4495 |                     while (i.hasNext()) {
4496 |                         XYAnnotation a = (XYAnnotation) i.next();
4497 |                         if (a instanceof XYAnnotationBoundsInfo) {
4498 |                             includedAnnotations.add(a);
4499 |                         }
4500 |                     }
4501 |             }
4502 |         }
4503 | 
4504 |         Iterator it = includedAnnotations.iterator();
4505 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
4506 |             XYAnnotationBoundsInfo xyabi = (XYAnnotationBoundsInfo) it.next();
4507 |             if (xyabi.getIncludeInDataBounds()) {
4508 |                 if (isDomainAxis) {
4509 |                     result = Range.combine(result, xyabi.getXRange());
4510 |                 }
4511 |                 else {
4512 |                     result = Range.combine(result, xyabi.getYRange());
4513 |                 }
4514 |             }
4515 |         }
4516 | 
4517 |         return result;
4518 | 
4519 |     }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.iterateDomainBounds(XYDataset,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 737-782
Documentation:
/**
     * Iterates over the items in an {@link XYDataset} to find
     * the range of x-values.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines, for an
     *          {@link IntervalXYDataset}, whether the x-interval or just the
     *          x-value is used to determine the overall range.
     *
     * @return The range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
737 |     public static Range iterateDomainBounds(XYDataset dataset,
738 |                                             boolean includeInterval) {
739 |         if (dataset == null) {
740 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'dataset' argument.");
741 |         }
742 |         double minimum = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
743 |         double maximum = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
744 |         int seriesCount = dataset.getSeriesCount();
745 |         double lvalue;
746 |         double uvalue;
747 |         if (includeInterval && dataset instanceof IntervalXYDataset) {
748 |             IntervalXYDataset intervalXYData = (IntervalXYDataset) dataset;
749 |             for (int series = 0; series < seriesCount; series++) {
750 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
751 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
752 |                     lvalue = intervalXYData.getStartXValue(series, item);
753 |                     uvalue = intervalXYData.getEndXValue(series, item);
754 |                     if (!Double.isNaN(lvalue)) {
755 |                         minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue);
756 |                     }
757 |                     if (!Double.isNaN(uvalue)) {
758 |                         maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue);
759 |                     }
760 |                 }
761 |             }
762 |         }
763 |         else {
764 |             for (int series = 0; series < seriesCount; series++) {
765 |                 int itemCount = dataset.getItemCount(series);
766 |                 for (int item = 0; item < itemCount; item++) {
767 |                     lvalue = dataset.getXValue(series, item);
768 |                     uvalue = lvalue;
769 |                     if (!Double.isNaN(lvalue)) {
770 |                         minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue);
771 |                         maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue);
772 |                     }
773 |                 }
774 |             }
775 |         }
776 |         if (minimum > maximum) {
777 |             return null;
778 |         }
779 |         else {
780 |             return new Range(minimum, maximum);
781 |         }
782 |     }

Original range: 872-909
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the range of values the renderer requires to display all the
     * items from the specified dataset.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param includeInterval  include the interval (if any) for the dataset?
     *
     * @return The range (<code>null</code> if the dataset is <code>null</code>
     *         or empty).
     *
     * @since 1.0.13
     */
Method code:
872 |     protected Range findRangeBounds(XYDataset dataset,
873 |             boolean includeInterval) {
874 |         if (dataset == null) {
875 |             return null;
876 |         }
877 |         if (getDataBoundsIncludesVisibleSeriesOnly()) {
878 |             List visibleSeriesKeys = new ArrayList();
879 |             int seriesCount = dataset.getSeriesCount();
880 |             for (int s = 0; s < seriesCount; s++) {
881 |                 if (isSeriesVisible(s)) {
882 |                     visibleSeriesKeys.add(dataset.getSeriesKey(s));
883 |                 }
884 |             }

886 |             // the current range of the x-axis...if there is one
887 |             Range xRange = null;
888 |             XYPlot p = getPlot();

890 |                 ValueAxis xAxis = null;
891 |                 int index = p.getIndexOf(this);
892 |                 if (index >= 0) {
893 |                     xAxis = plot.getDomainAxisForDataset(index);
894 |                 }

897 |                 }
898 |             }

900 |                 xRange = new Range(Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY,
901 |                         Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY);
902 |             }
903 |             return DatasetUtilities.findRangeBounds(dataset,
904 |                     visibleSeriesKeys, xRange, includeInterval);
905 |         }
906 |         else {
907 |             return DatasetUtilities.findRangeBounds(dataset, includeInterval);
908 |         }
909 |     }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.Plot.notifyListeners(PlotChangeEvent)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/Plot.java
Original range: 959-971
Documentation:
/**
     * Notifies all registered listeners that the plot has been modified.
     *
     * @param event  information about the change event.
     */
Method code:
959 |     public void notifyListeners(PlotChangeEvent event) {
960 |         // if the 'notify' flag has been switched to false, we don't notify
961 |         // the listeners
962 |         if (!this.notify) {
963 |             return;
964 |         }
965 |         Object[] listeners = this.listenerList.getListenerList();
966 |         for (int i = listeners.length - 2; i >= 0; i -= 2) {
967 |             if (listeners[i] == PlotChangeListener.class) {
968 |                 ((PlotChangeListener) listeners[i + 1]).plotChanged(event);
969 |             }
970 |         }
971 |     }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.SerialDate.lastDayOfMonth(int,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/SerialDate.java
Original range: 542-555
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the number of the last day of the month, taking into account
     * leap years.
     *
     * @param month  the month.
     * @param yyyy  the year (in the range 1900 to 9999).
     *
     * @return the number of the last day of the month.
     */
Method code:
542 |     public static int lastDayOfMonth(final int month, final int yyyy) {
543 | 
544 |         final int result = LAST_DAY_OF_MONTH[month];
545 |         if (month != FEBRUARY) {
546 |             return result;
547 |         }
548 |         else if (isLeapYear(yyyy)) {
549 |             return result + 1;
550 |         }
551 |         else {
552 |             return result;
553 |         }
554 | 
555 |     }

Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/XYPlot.java
Original range: 1478-1487
Documentation:
/**
     * Maps the specified dataset to the axes in the list.  Note that the
     * conversion of data values into Java2D space is always performed using
     * the first axis in the list.
     *
     * @param index  the dataset index (zero-based).
     * @param axisIndices  the axis indices (<code>null</code> permitted).
     *
     * @since 1.0.12
     */
Method code:
1478 |     public void mapDatasetToDomainAxes(int index, List axisIndices) {
1479 |         if (index < 0) {
1480 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Requires 'index' >= 0.");
1481 |         }
1482 |         checkAxisIndices(axisIndices);
1483 |         Integer key = new Integer(index);
1484 |         this.datasetToDomainAxesMap.put(key, new ArrayList(axisIndices));
1485 |         // fake a dataset change event to update axes...
1486 |         datasetChanged(new DatasetChangeEvent(this, getDataset(index)));
1487 |     }

===== M0409 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.time.Day.getLastMillisecond(Calendar)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/time/Day.java
Original range: 342-351
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the last millisecond of the day, evaluated using the supplied
     * calendar (which determines the time zone).
     *
     * @param calendar  calendar to use (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The end of the day as milliseconds since 01-01-1970.
     *
     * @throws NullPointerException if <code>calendar</code> is
     *     <code>null</code>.
     */
Method code:
342 |     public long getLastMillisecond(Calendar calendar) {
343 |         int year = this.serialDate.getYYYY();
344 |         int month = this.serialDate.getMonth();
345 |         int day = this.serialDate.getDayOfMonth();
346 |         calendar.clear();
347 |         calendar.set(year, month - 1, day, 23, 59, 59);
348 |         calendar.set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 999);
349 |         //return calendar.getTimeInMillis();  // this won't work for JDK 1.3
350 |         return calendar.getTime().getTime();
351 |     }

     * Notifies all registered listeners that the axis has changed.
     * The AxisChangeEvent provides information about the change.
